\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert 0.5I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left(0.5-\eta\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

To provide a bound for
(A_{2})
, observe that

0.5I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}=0.5I-\eta\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}.

The next step in our proof is to show that, with the choice of
\eta\lesssim 1/\sigma_{1}
, the eigenvalues of
\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
are nonnegative. We prove this by showing that the eigenvalues of
\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
are close to those of
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
. First, note that
I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)
is positive definite due to our choice of step-size. Therefore, the matrix
D=\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)^{1/2}
is well-defined and invertable. Hence, for any
1\leq i\leq r
, we have

\lambda_{i}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)=\lambda_{i}\left(D^{-1}\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}D\right)=\lambda_{i}\left(DS_{t}S_{t}^{\top}D\right).

To proceed with our argument, we first present a relative perturbation bound for symmetric matrices.

Lemma 21

(Relative Perturbation Bound,
Eisenstat and Ipsen 1995
)
.

Let
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
be a symmetric matrix with eigenvalues
\lambda_{1}\geq\dots\geq\lambda_{d}
. Moreover, suppose
R
is a non-singular matrix. Let
\hat{\lambda}_{1}\geq\dots\geq\hat{\lambda}_{d}
be the eigenvalues of
Y=R^{\top}XR
. Then, we have

\left|\lambda_{i}-\hat{\lambda}_{i}\right|\leq|\lambda_{i}|\left\lVert I-R^{\top}R\right\rVert,\quad\text{for all }i.

Invoking Lemma
21
with
X=S_{t}S_{t}
and
Y=DS_{t}S_{t}D
results in

\displaystyle{\left|\lambda_{i}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)-\lambda_{i}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\lambda_{i}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\right|\left\lVert I-D^{2}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left|\lambda_{i}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\right|\left\lVert I-\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\eta\left|\lambda_{i}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\right|\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 0.5\left|\lambda_{i}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\right|.

for every
i=1,2,\dots,r
. The above inequality implies that
\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\asymp\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)
, and therefore,
\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\geq 0
. This leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert 0.5I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert 0.5I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 0.5-\lambda_{\min}\left(\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=0.5-\lambda_{\min}\left(\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=0.5-\eta\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\leq 0.5,

where in the last inequality, we used the fact that
\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(I+\eta\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\geq 0
. Combined with the definition of
(A_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert 0.5I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 0.5\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

which in turn implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert(A)\right\rVert\leq\left(1-\eta\lambda_{\min}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\right)\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

Combining the derived upper bounds for
(A)
,
(B)
, and
(C)
completes the proof for the signal dynamics.
\hfill\square

Bounding
\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

Recalling the update rules (
80
) and (
81
), we have

\displaystyle S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}+\eta(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}-\eta S_{t}(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t})E_{t}^{\top}}_{(A)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t})E_{t}^{\top}}_{(B)}-\underbrace{\eta S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E^{\top}_{t}}_{(C)}+\underbrace{\eta^{2}S_{t}E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)E_{t}^{\top}}_{(D)}.

We provide separate bounds for the individual terms in the above equality. First, observe that
\left\lVert(C)\right\rVert\leq\eta\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
. Moreover, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert(B)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t})E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

where, in the last inequality, we used the assumption that
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
and
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert(D)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\eta^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

where, in (a), we used
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\succeq E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\succeq 0
. Finally, we provide an upper bound for
(A)
.

\displaystyle\left\lVert(A)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\eta\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\eta\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+1-\eta\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

Combining the derived bounds for (A), (B), (C), and (D) concludes the proof.
\hfill\square

Bounding
\left\lVert E_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

Due to the update rule for
E_{t+1}
, one can write

\displaystyle E_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}-2\eta E_{t}\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)E_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}E_{t}\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)^{2}E_{t}^{\top}

(83)

\displaystyle=E_{t}\left(I-2\eta\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)+\eta^{2}\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)^{2}\right)E_{t}^{\top}.

On the other hand,
\eta\lesssim 1/\sigma_{1}
,
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
, and
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
imply that

I-\eta E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}\succ I-2\eta\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)+\eta^{2}\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)^{2}\succ 0.

(84)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert E_{t}\left(I-2\eta\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)+\eta^{2}\left(E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)^{2}\right)E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t}\left(I-\eta E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}-\eta E_{t}E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left(1-\eta\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

where in the last inequality, we used the fact that
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\sigma_{1}
.

Bounding
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

First, recall that

\displaystyle S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta\Sigma S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle-2\eta S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)}

\displaystyle\preceq

\displaystyle\underbrace{S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta\Sigma S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)}_{(A)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\eta^{2}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}+\eta^{2}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)}_{(B)},

where the last inequality follows by noting that
{-2\eta S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}}\preceq 0
. Now, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\left\lVert(B)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\eta^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(\sigma_{1}+\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3\eta^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(\sigma_{1}+\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right).

It remains to provide an upper bound for
(A)
. One can write

\displaystyle(A)=

\displaystyle\underbrace{S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\eta\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{3}+\Sigma S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma\right)}_{({A_{1}})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\eta\Sigma S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\Sigma}_{(A_{2})}.

For (
A_{1}
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert(A_{1})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\eta\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{3}\right\rVert+\eta^{2}\left\lVert\Sigma S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\eta\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{3}\right\rVert+\eta^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-2\eta\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\eta^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{3}+\eta^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

where (a) follows from the fact that
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
,
\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}
,
\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{3}
share the same eigenvectors, and the assumption
\eta\lesssim 1/\sigma_{1}
. On the other hand, one can easily verify that

\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert\leq 2\eta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(I-\eta S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right\rVert\leq 2\eta\sigma_{1}\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-\eta\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}\right).

Combining the upper bounds for (A) and (B) leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left((1+\eta\sigma_{1})^{2}+3\eta^{2}\sigma_{1}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-2\eta\left(1+\eta\sigma_{1}-1.5\eta\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{3}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\left((1+2.001\sigma_{1}\right)\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-2\eta\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\eta^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{3}=f\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right),

where, in (a), we used the assumption
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
and
\eta\lesssim 1/\sigma_{1}
. Now, let us define the function

f(x):=\eta^{2}x^{3}-2\eta x^{2}+\left(1+2.001\sigma_{1}\right)x.

It is easy to see that
f(x)
is increasing within the interval
x\leq{1}/{4\eta}
. On the other hand, we have
1.01\sigma_{1}\leq{1}/{4\eta}
due to our choice of
\eta
. Therefore, we have

\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq f\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)\leq f\left(1.01\sigma_{1}\right).

On the other hand, simple calculation reveals that

\displaystyle f(1.01\sigma_{1})=\eta^{2}(1.01\sigma_{1})^{3}-2\eta(1.01\sigma_{1})^{2}+\left(1+2.001\sigma_{1}\right)(1.01\sigma_{1})\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}.

for
\eta\leq{c}/{\sigma_{1}}
with sufficiently small constant
c
. This completes the proof.
\hfill\square

C.3
Proof of Lemma
5

We prove this lemma by induction on
t
. First, due to our choice of the initial point in Theorem
6
, we have
\left\lVert E_{0}E_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\alpha^{2}
,
\left\lVert S_{0}S_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}
and
S_{0}S_{0}^{\top}\succ 0
. Now, suppose that (
34
), (
35
), and (
36
) hold for
t
. Then, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert E_{0}E_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\alpha^{2},

(85)

due to the decreasing nature of
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
. Therefore, Proposition
3
can be invoked to show that
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}
. Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-\eta\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}

(86)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta t+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}-\frac{\eta}{\left(\eta t+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta(t+1)+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}.

On the other hand, Proposition
2
can be used to show that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+\eta^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)-2\eta\left(1+\eta\sigma_{r}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-2\eta\sigma_{1}\left(1+\eta\sigma_{r}\right)+\eta^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle>0,

where in the first inequality, we used
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{r}
, which follows from
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\alpha^{2}
and our choice of
\alpha
. Moreover, the last inequality follows from our choice of
\eta
.
\hfill\square

Appendix D
Proofs of Empirical Loss with Noiseless Measurements

D.1
Preliminaries

For simplicity, we define
\Delta_{t}=U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}
.
Before presenting the proofs for the empirical loss, we first introduce a key decomposition.
Recall the update rule

U_{t+1}=U_{t}-2\eta_{t}Q_{t}U_{t}:=\tilde{U}_{t+1}+R_{t}U_{t},

(87)

where
\eta_{t}=\frac{\eta}{2}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}|\left\langle A_{i},\Delta_{t}\right\rangle|
and
Q_{t}\in\mathcal{Q}(\Delta_{t})
. Moreover, denote
\tilde{U}_{t+1}=U_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Delta_{t}U_{t}
, where
\bar{\eta}_{t}=\eta\varphi(\Delta_{t})^{2}
is the update rule obtained from the expected loss, and
R_{t}U_{t}
with
R_{t}=\bar{\eta}_{t}\Delta_{t}-2\eta_{t}Q_{t}
is the residual caused by the deviation of the empirical loss from its expectation. Finally, we define
\tilde{S}_{t}=V^{\top}\tilde{U}_{t}
, and
\tilde{E}_{t}=V_{\perp}^{\top}\tilde{U}_{t}
.

Lemma 22
.

Suppose that the measurements satisfy Sign-RIP with parameters
\left(4r,\delta,\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right)
. Then, we have
\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}
.

Proof.

One can write

\Delta_{t}=\underbrace{V\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)V^{\top}+VS_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}+V_{\perp}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}}_{\text{rank-$4r$}}+\underbrace{V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}}_{\text{small perturbation}}.

Note that
\left\lVert V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
. Therefore,
\Delta_{t}
is an
\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
-approximate rank-
4r
matrix. One can write

\displaystyle R_{t}=

\displaystyle\eta\varphi(\Delta_{t})^{2}\Delta_{t}-2\eta_{t}Q_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\eta}{2}\varphi(\Delta)\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}-\eta_{t}\right)Q_{t}+\left(\frac{\eta}{2}\varphi(\Delta)\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}-\eta_{t}\right)Q_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{2}\varphi(\Delta_{t})\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left(\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}-Q_{t}\right)+\frac{\eta}{2}\varphi(\Delta_{t})\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left(\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}-Q_{t}^{\top}\right)

Due to the above decomposition, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq{2\left|{\frac{\eta}{2}\varphi(\Delta)\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}-\eta_{t}}\right|\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}+{\eta\varphi(\Delta_{t})\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\varphi(X)\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}-Q_{t}\right\rVert}.

(88)

First, note that
\left|{(\eta/2)\varphi(\Delta)\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}-\eta_{t}}\right|\leq\delta(\eta/2)\varphi(\Delta_{t})\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}
due to Lemma
20
. Moreover, due to Sign-RIP, we have
\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert\leq(1+\delta)\varphi(\Delta_{t})
and
\left\lVert\varphi(X)\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}-Q_{t}\right\rVert\leq\delta\varphi(\Delta_{t})
.
Combining these upper bounds with (
88
) completes the proof.
∎

D.2
Proof of Proposition
4

Due to the proposed decomposition (
87
), one can write

\displaystyle S_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\tilde{S}_{t+1}+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}.

(89)

Our main goal is to show that the minimum eigenvalue of
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
follows that of
\tilde{S}_{t}\tilde{S}_{t}^{\top}
—which has been characterized in Lemma
2
—plus an additional deviation caused by the term
V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}
.
Similar to the proof of Proposition
2
, we characterize the growth rate of
\lambda_{\min}(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top})
by first resorting to a more tractable lower bound. Adopting the notation introduced in Appendix
D
, consider the following auxiliary matrix

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle:=\left(I+\Xi\right)\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(I+\Xi\right)^{\top}=\left(I+\Xi\right)V^{\top}\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Delta_{t}\right)U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Delta_{t}\right)\left(I+\Xi\right)^{\top},

where
\Xi:=V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Note that, according to Lemma
5
, we have
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\succ 0
due to our assumption
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\succ 0
. Therefore,
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
is invertible, and hence, the matrix
\Xi
is well-defined. On the other hand, it is easy to see that
\Xi
satisfies

V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}=\Xi\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}.

(90)

Based on the above equality, one can write

\displaystyle S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}-M

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V+\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)

(91)

\displaystyle-\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}-\Xi\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\Xi^{\top}+\Xi\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\Xi^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V-V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V

\displaystyle=V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\left(I-\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}\right)U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V

\displaystyle=V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\mathsf{P}_{\tilde{S}+1}^{\perp}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V

\displaystyle\succeq 0.

Therefore, we have
\lambda_{\min}(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top})\geq\lambda_{\min}(M)
. Our next goal is to provide a lower bound for
\lambda_{\min}(M)
. To this goal, we will show that the minimum eigenvalue of
M
is close to that of
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
. Combined with the minimum eigenvalue dynamics of
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
presented in Proposition
2
, this completes the proof.

To show that
\lambda_{\min}(M)\approx\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top})
, we will use the relative perturbation bound presented in Lemma
21
. To this goal, first we need to provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert
.

Bounding
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert
.

One can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

(92)

\displaystyle=\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)\right)^{-1}\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}2\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}6\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert,

where in (a), we used the fact that
\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
due to our assumptions on
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
, and our choice of
\eta
. Moreover, (b) follows from Lemma
22
. Now, note that

\displaystyle\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=V\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+V_{\perp}\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

(93)

\displaystyle=V+V_{\perp}\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{U}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 1+\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert.

In order to provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert
, it suffices to bound
\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
. To this goal, first we present the following technical lemma, the proof of which can be found in Appendix
F.2
.

Lemma 23
.

The following statements hold:

-

\frac{1}{2}\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 2
and
\frac{1}{3}\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left({S}_{t+1}{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 3
;

-

\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 3\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
.

The second statement of the above lemma connects
\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
to
\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
which, according to our assumption, is upper bounded by
1/3
. This upper bound, together with (
92
) implies that

\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\leq 12\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

On the other hand, applying the relative perturbation bound to
M
and
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
implies that

\displaystyle\left|\lambda_{\min}(M)-\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top})\right|\leq

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top})\left\lVert I-(I+\Xi)(I+\Xi)^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 36\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right).

This in turn implies that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}({S}_{t+1}{S}_{t+1}^{\top})

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}(M)

\displaystyle\geq(1-36\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F})\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(\left(1+\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}\right)^{2}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-72\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle-2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(1+\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2},

which completes the proof.
\hfill\square

D.3
Proof of Proposition
5

Bounding
\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

Recall that
S_{t+1}=\tilde{S}_{t+1}+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}=\Sigma-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\underbrace{-V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}-\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V-V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V}_{\text{deviation}}.

(94)

The main idea behind our proof is to first control the norm of the deviation term, and then invoke Proposition
3
for
\Sigma-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
.
One can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert,

where in the last inequality, we used Lemma
22
. Moreover,
\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}+S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)\right)\left(S^{\top}V^{\top}+E^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\right)S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}

\displaystyle+S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}.

Note that
\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\right\rVert\leq 2
due to our choice of
\eta
and our assumption on
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq 6\sigma_{1},

(95)

where we used our assumptions on
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
. Combining the above two inequalities leads to

\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert=\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 18\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

(96)

Using a similar technique, we have

\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert\leq 9\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta^{2}\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F},

(97)

where we used the assumed upper bounds on
\eta
and
\delta
, and the fact that
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\sigma_{1}
,
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}
, and
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}
, which in turn implies
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}
due to Lemma
3
. Moreover, we have already shown in Proposition
3
that

\left\lVert\Sigma-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+5\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}.

Hence, combining the above inequalities leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+5\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+37\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

Bounding
\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

First, note that

\displaystyle S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}+\underbrace{V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}+\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}}_{\text{deviation}}.

Similar to the signal term, the main idea behind our proof is to first control the norm of the deviation term, and then invoke Proposition
3
for
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}
.
We first provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert.

To bound
\left\lVert U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert U_{t}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert U_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 5{\sigma_{1}},

where we used the assumption
\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sqrt{\sigma_{1}}
and
\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{\sigma_{1}}
. Similar to (
97
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert S_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}+S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sqrt{\sigma_{1}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F},

and

\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq 10\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta^{2}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

Moreover, we have already shown in Proposition
3
that

\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{E}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

(98)

Combining the above inequalities leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+22\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

Bounding
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

First, note that

S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}=\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+\underbrace{V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}+S_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V}_{\text{deviation}}.

Similar to our previous arguments, we will provide an upper bound on the deviation term, and then resort to Proposition
3
to provide an upper bound for
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
. First, note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}VS_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}E_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\right),

(99)

where we used Lemma
22
in the last inequality. On the other hand

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert S_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(1+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)+\left\lVert S_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert+\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 3\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

Similarly, we have

\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 3\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

The above two inequalities combined with (
99
) results in

\displaystyle 2\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 216\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}^{2}\sqrt{r}\delta.

which follows from our assumption on
\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert
,
\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert
and
\delta
. Similarly, one can show that

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}^{2}\sqrt{r}\delta.

Therefore, the norm of the deviation term is upper bounded by
217\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}^{2}\sqrt{r}\delta
. Moreover, Proposition
3
implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left(1+2\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}+4\sigma_{1}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\right)\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{3},

which in turn leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left(1+2\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}+4\sigma_{1}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\right)\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert-2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{3}+217\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}^{2}\sqrt{r}\delta.

The rest of the proof is analogous to that of Proposition
3
, and hence, omitted for brevity.
\hfill\square

D.4
Proof of Proposition
6

Bounding
\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert
:

To provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert=\left\lVert E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}\right\rVert
in terms of
\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert=\left\lVert E_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\right\rVert
, it is crucial to characterize the relationship between the projection operators
\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}
and
\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}
. To this goal, we decompose
\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}
as follows

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}=\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}+\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}.

Based on the above decomposition,
G_{t+1}
can be written as

\displaystyle G_{t+1}=\underbrace{E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}}_{(A)}+\underbrace{E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}}_{(B)}.

We first study
(A)
. Let
M_{t}D_{t}N_{t}^{\top}
be the singular value decomposition of
S_{t}
, where
M_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
and
N_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r^{\prime}\times r}
are (row) orthonormal matrices, and
D_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
is a diagonal matrix collecting the singular values of
S_{t}
. Based on this definition, we have
\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}=N_{t}N_{t}^{\top}
.
On the other hand, we have
S_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}=-S_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}
, which is equivalent to

S_{t+1}N_{t}N_{t}^{\top}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}=-S_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}.

(100)

Our next technical lemma shows that
S_{t+1}N_{t}
is invertible.

Lemma 24
.

The matrix
S_{t+1}N_{t}
is invertible.

The proof of this lemma can be found in Appendix
F.3
. Lemma (
24
) combined with (
100
) leads to

N_{t}^{\top}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}=-\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle(A)=

\displaystyle E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E_{t+1}N_{t}N_{t}^{\top}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{-E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}}_{(A_{1})}-\underbrace{E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\left(S_{t+1}-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}}_{(A_{2})}.

We first control
(A_{1})
. Observe that

\tilde{S}_{t+1}\mathsf{P}^{\perp}_{S_{t}}=\left(S_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)\mathsf{P}^{\perp}_{S_{t}}=-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}G_{t}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle(A_{1})

\displaystyle=\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}.

(101)

On the other hand, note that
E_{t+1}=E_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}
.
Hence, we have

(B)=\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}.

(102)

Combining equations (
101
) and (
102
), we obtain

G_{t+1}=\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}+(A_{2}),

which results in

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C)}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert.

Therefore, it remains to control the terms
\left\lVert(C)\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert
.
First, we provide an upper bound on
\left\lVert(C)\right\rVert
. Define
S_{t+1}N_{t}=\left(I+\Xi\right)S_{t}N_{t}
, where
\Xi=S_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}-I
(note that
S_{t}N_{t}=M_{t}D_{t}
which implies that
S_{t}N_{t}
is invertible). Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert(C)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{1})}+\underbrace{\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert(I+\Xi)^{-1}-I\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{2})}.

(103)

It is shown in the proof of Lemma
24
that
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\leq 1/2
. Therefore, one can write
\left\lVert(I+\Xi)^{-1}-I\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(I+\Xi\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
. To provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
, one can write

\displaystyle E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=E_{t+1}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(E_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\right)S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)H_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)H_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}V+V_{\perp}^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}H_{t},

where
H_{t}=E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert(1+\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+4\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert C_{2}\right\rVert\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left(\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert.

To control
\left\lVert(C_{1})\right\rVert
, we use triangle inequality to arrive at the following decomposition

\displaystyle(C_{1})

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{11})}+\underbrace{\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{12})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{13})}+\underbrace{\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert R_{t}U_{t}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert}_{(C_{14})}.

It is easy to see that

\left\lVert(C_{11})\right\rVert=\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1
, due to the assumed upper bound on
\bar{\eta}_{t}
and
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
. Moreover, one can verify that
\left\lVert(C_{12})\right\rVert\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}
,
\left\lVert(C_{13})\right\rVert\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}
, and
\left\lVert(C_{14})\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left(1+\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
. Combining the derived upper bounds for
\left\lVert C_{1}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert C_{2}\right\rVert
, we have

(C)\leq 1+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(2\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}+7\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+6\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right).

To complete the proof, it remains to provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert
. First, note that
\left(S_{t+1}-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}=V^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}G_{t}
. Given this equality, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert A_{2}\right\rVert\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}V^{\top}R_{t}V_{\perp}G_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}^{\perp}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Therefore, it suffices to provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}N_{t}\left(S_{t+1}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert(I+\Xi)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert E_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2.

Combining the above inequalities leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert(A_{2})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Finally, combining the derived upper bounds for
(C)
and
(A_{2})
gives rise to the following inequalities

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+2\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}+6\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+7\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+2\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}+2\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+7\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Bounding
\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert:

Similar to the previous part, we use the decomposition
\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}=\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}+\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}
to write

\displaystyle F_{t+1}=E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}=\underbrace{E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}}_{(A)}+\underbrace{E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}}_{(B)}.

First, we provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert(B)\right\rVert
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert{({B})}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\left(\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)G_{t}+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{t}U_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}^{\perp}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{t}V_{\perp}G_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert.

(104)

To control
\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert
, we use the following technical lemma.

Lemma 25

(Theorem 2.4,
Chen et al. 2016
)
.

Let
A\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
, and
B=A+E\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
have the same rank. Then, we have

\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{A}-\mathsf{P}_{B}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert EA^{\dagger}\right\rVert\vee\left\lVert EB^{\dagger}\right\rVert.

Due to Proposition
4
and our assumptions, we have
\lambda_{\min}(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top})\geq\lambda_{\min}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})>0
, and hence, both
S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}
and
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}
are rank-
r
. Invoking Lemma
25
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathsf{P}_{S_{t+1}}-\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(S_{t+1}-S_{t}\right)S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

(105)

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}+S_{t}E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}\right)+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\right)S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left(1+\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\right)

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+6\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert,

where in the last inequality, we used the following auxiliary lemma.

Lemma 26
.

If
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\geq\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
, then we have
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 5\sqrt{r}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
.

Proof.

Recall the signal-residual decomposition

\Delta_{t}=V\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)V^{\top}+VS_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}+V_{\perp}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+V_{\perp}F_{t}F_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}+V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}.

One can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\left(\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\rVert+2\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert F_{t}F_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)+\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=4\sqrt{r}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 5\sqrt{r}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert,

which completes the proof.
∎

Combining (
105
) with (
104
) leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert(B)\right\rVert\leq 6\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Next, we will provide an upper bound for
(A)
. One can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({A}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert E_{t+1}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(E_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}\left(E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}+S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{t}U_{t}\right)\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert F_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}F_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}F_{t}S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{t}U_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert.

(106)

The first term in the above inequality can be bounded as follows

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}F_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}F_{t}S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(0.5I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right)F_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert F_{t}\left(0.5I-\bar{\eta}_{t}S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\lVert 0.5I-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert 0.5I-\bar{\eta}_{t}S^{\top}_{t}S_{t}\right\rVert\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{t}U_{t}\mathsf{P}_{S_{t}}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\left(\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert\right)\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left(\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({A}\right)\right\rVert\leq\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert.

Finally, combining the derived upper bounds for
(A)
and
(B)
leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert+6\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert,

which completes the proof.
\hfill\square

D.5
Proof of Lemma
6

Recall that
U_{0}=\alpha B
, where
BB^{\top}
is the best rank-
r^{\prime}
approximation of
C\in\frac{1}{2m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathrm{Sign}\left(y_{i}\right)\left({A_{i}+A_{i}^{\top}}\right)
. Since
\mathrm{rank}(X^{\star})=r
, Sign-RIP implies

\left\lVert C-\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert\leq\varphi(X^{\star})\delta.

Note that
BB^{\top}
is the best rank-
r^{\prime}
approximation of
C
. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert BB^{\top}-\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert BB^{\top}-C\right\rVert+\left\lVert C-\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left|\lambda_{r^{\prime}+1}(C)\right|+\varphi(X^{\star})\delta

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert C-\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert+\varphi(X^{\star})\delta

\displaystyle\leq 2\varphi(X^{\star})\delta.

Therefore, based on the definition of
U_{0}
, we have

\left\lVert U_{0}U_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert\leq 2\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\delta.

Given this bound, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{0}S_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{\Sigma}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert V^{\top}\left(U_{0}U_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)V\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert U_{0}U_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\delta.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert S_{0}E_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert V^{\top}\left(U_{0}U_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)V_{\perp}\right\rVert\leq 2\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\delta,

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{0}E_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert V_{\perp}^{\top}\left(U_{0}U_{0}^{\top}-\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\frac{X^{\star}}{\left\lVert X^{\star}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)V_{\perp}\right\rVert\leq 2\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\delta.

This completes the proof.
\hfill\square

D.6
Proof of Lemma
7

We prove this lemma by induction on
t
. First, due to Lemma
6
, it is easy to verify that (
45
)-(
48
) hold for
t=0
. Now, suppose that (
45
)-(
47
) are satisfied for
t<T_{end}
. Moreover, without loss of generality, we assume that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\gtrsim d\alpha^{2-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{r}\kappa^{2}\delta)}
for every
0\leq t\leq T_{end}
; otherwise, the statement of the lemma holds. Together with the induction hypothesis on
\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert
, this implies that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\zeta
, for
\zeta>0
defined in Propositions
4
,
5
, and
6
.

Bounding
\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert
:

In order to apply Proposition
6
, first we verify its assumptions. One can write
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert=\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
. Therefore, we have
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\sigma_{1}
, due to the induction hypothesis on
\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert
. On the other hand,
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}
and
\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 1/3
due to our induction hypothesis. It remains to show that
(4r,\delta,\varepsilon,\mathcal{S})
-Sign-RIP with
\varepsilon\geq\sqrt{d}\bar{\varphi}\alpha
implies
(4r,\delta,\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2},\mathcal{S})
-Sign-RIP. To show this, first note that
(4r,\delta,\varepsilon_{1},\mathcal{S})
-Sign-RIP implies
(4r,\delta,\varepsilon_{2},\mathcal{S})
-Sign-RIP, for any
\varepsilon_{2}\leq\varepsilon_{1}
. Using this fact, it suffices to show that
\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\bar{\varphi}\alpha\lesssim\varepsilon
, which is immediate due to our induction hypothesis on
\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert
, and our choice of
\delta
. Therefore, Proposition
6
holds and we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert S_{t}\right\rVert+6\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

(107)

Due to our induction hypothesis, (
107
) can be simplified as

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+75\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\right)\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+80\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}^{1.5}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta+30\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+100\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}^{1.5}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta+30\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert F_{t}\right\rVert+100\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}^{1.5}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta+30\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\sqrt{\alpha\bar{\varphi}\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\bar{\varphi}^{2}\left(100\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}^{1.5}\delta+30\sigma_{1}\sqrt{\alpha\bar{\varphi}\delta}\right)(t+2),

where in the first inequality, we use Lemma
26
and the induction hypothesis on
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
. Moreover, in the last inequality, we used the induction hypothesis on
\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert
.

Bounding
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
:

Similar to our previous argument, it is easy to verify that the assumptions of Proposition
5
are satisfied at iteration
t
. Therefore,
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.01\sigma_{1}
readily follows from Proposition
5
.

Bounding
\left\lVert U_{t+1}U_{t+1}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right\rVert
:

Note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert U_{t+1}U_{t+1}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert E_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}\leq 0.5\sigma_{1}.

where the last inequality is due to our choice of
\delta
and
\alpha
. Similarly, we can show that
\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\sigma_{1}
. This together with
\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+\sigma_{1}\leq 2.01\sigma_{1}
leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert U_{t+1}U_{t+1}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right\rVert\leq 5\sigma_{1}.

Establishing
S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\succ 0
:

The proof follows directly from the application of Proposition
4
. The details are omitted due to its similarity to the proof of (
36
) in Lemma
5
.

Bounding
\left\lVert E_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
:

To streamline the proof, let
H_{t}=E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Our goal is to prove
\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\leq 1/3
by showing the following recursive relationship.

Lemma 27
.

For every
0\leq s\leq t
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert\leq(1-c\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r})\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert+c^{\prime}\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta,

(108)

where
c,c^{\prime}>0
are some universal constants.

The proof of Lemma
27
can be found in Appendix
F.4
. Equipped with this lemma, we are ready to derive the desired result.
First, due to Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert H_{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\left\lVert E_{0}S_{0}^{\top}\right\rVert}{\lambda_{\min}(S_{0}S_{0}^{\top})}\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}\delta{\varphi}(X^{\star})}{\alpha^{2}\varphi(X^{\star})\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{r}\kappa}-2\delta\right)}\leq 4\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta,

provided that
\delta\leq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{r}\kappa}
. On the other hand, (
108
) implies that

\displaystyle\left\lVert H_{t+1}\right\rVert-\frac{c^{\prime}}{c}\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\leq(1-c\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r})^{t+1}\left(\left\lVert H_{0}\right\rVert-\frac{c^{\prime}}{c}\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\right)\leq\left\lVert H_{0}\right\rVert+\frac{c^{\prime}}{c}\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta.

Therefore, due to our choice of
\delta
, we have

\left\lVert H_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq\left(4\sqrt{r}\kappa+\frac{2c^{\prime}}{c}\sqrt{r}\kappa\right)\delta\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\leq 1/3.

Bounding
\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert
:

Due to Proposition
6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq\left(1+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(2\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}+6\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}(S_{t}S_{t})^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)+7\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Moreover, one can write

\displaystyle\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim r\sigma_{1}^{3}\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta^{2}t^{2}+\sigma_{1}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\alpha\bar{\varphi}\delta t^{2}\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right).

Similarly, it can be shown that

\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert E_{t}\right\rVert^{4}\vee\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\vee\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right).

Therefore, for some universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+C\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+C\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)^{t+1}\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+C\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)^{C^{\prime}\frac{\log({1}/{\alpha})}{\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}}}\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(C^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\sqrt{r}\kappa^{2}\delta\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{-\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{r}\kappa^{2}\delta\right)}\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{1-\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{r}\kappa^{2}\delta\right)}\sqrt{\bar{\varphi}\delta},

where in the last inequality, we used the upper bound
\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert E_{0}\right\rVert
and Lemma
6
.

Appendix E
Proofs of Outlier Noise Model

E.1
Preliminaries

Given the update rule
U_{t+1}=U_{t}-2\eta_{t}Q_{t}U_{t}
, we consider the following decomposition

\displaystyle U_{t+1}=\tilde{U}_{t+1}+R_{t}U_{t},\quad\text{where}\quad\tilde{U}_{t+1}=U_{t}-\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}U_{t},\ R_{t}=\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}-2\eta_{t}Q_{t},

(109)

In the above decomposition,
\tilde{U}_{t+1}
resembles one iteration of GD on
\bar{f}_{\ell_{2}}(U)
with the “effective” step-size
\frac{\eta\rho^{t}}{2\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}
. Moreover, the term
R_{t}U_{t}
captures the deviation of SubGM and GD. Similar to the noiseless setting, the main idea behind our proof technique is to show that
R_{t}
remains small throughout the iterations of SubGM, and consequently, SubGM behaves similar to GD.
To this goal, we first provide an upper bound on
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}
in terms of
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
.

Lemma 28
.

Suppose that
\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
. Then, we have
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2(1+\sqrt{r})\left\lVert\Delta\right\rVert
.

Proof.

Due to our proposed signal-residual decomposition, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\underbrace{V\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)V^{\top}+VS_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}+V_{\perp}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+V_{\perp}F_{t}F_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}}_{\text{rank-$4r$}}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\underbrace{V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}}_{\text{small norm}}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{4r}\left\lVert{V\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)V^{\top}+VS_{t}E_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}+V_{\perp}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}V^{\top}+V_{\perp}F_{t}F_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}}\right\rVert+\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{4r}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert+\sqrt{4r}\left\lVert V_{\perp}G_{t}G_{t}^{\top}V_{\perp}^{\top}\right\rVert+\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\sqrt{r})\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert,

where the last inequality follows from the assumption
4r\leq d
and
\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
. This completes the proof.
∎

Equipped with this technical lemma, we next provide an upper bound on
\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert
.

Lemma 29
.

Suppose that the measurements satisfy
\left(4r,\delta,\varepsilon,\mathcal{S}\right)
-Sign-RIP with
\delta<\frac{1}{4(1+\sqrt{r})}
,
\varepsilon=\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
,
\mathcal{S}=\left\{X:\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}\geq\zeta\right\}
for
\zeta=\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\vee\sqrt{d}\right)
, and
\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert
. Then, we have
\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq 8(1+\sqrt{r})\eta\rho^{t}\delta
.

Proof.

One can write

\displaystyle R_{t}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}-\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}Q_{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}\left(Q_{t}-\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)-\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}\varphi(\Delta_{t})}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\Delta_{t}+\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}\left(Q_{t}-\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)+\frac{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert-\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}.

The above equality implies that

\displaystyle\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}\left\lVert Q_{t}-{\varphi(\Delta_{t})}\frac{\Delta_{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert+2\eta\rho^{t}\cdot\frac{\left|\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert-\varphi(\Delta_{t})\frac{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\right|}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}

(110)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert}\right)2\eta\rho^{t}\varphi(\Delta_{t})\delta,

where in the last inequality, we used Sign-RIP. Next, we provide an upper bound for
1/\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert
.
Due to Sign-RIP, we have
\left\lVert Q_{t}\right\rVert\geq\left(\frac{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}-\delta\right)\varphi(\Delta_{t})
. On the other hand, due to Lemma
28
, we have
\frac{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}}\leq\frac{1}{2(1+\sqrt{r})}
. Combining these inequalities with (
110
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{2}{\frac{1}{2(1+\sqrt{r})}-\delta}\cdot\eta\rho^{t}\delta\leq 8(1+\sqrt{r})\eta\rho^{t}\delta,

where the last inequality is due to the assumption
\delta\leq\frac{1}{4(1+\sqrt{r})}
.
∎

E.2
Proof of Proposition
7

The proof is almost a line-by-line reconstruction of the proof of Proposition
4
in Appendix
D.2
. For brevity, we only provide a sketch of the proof. Similar to (
89
), one can write

\displaystyle S_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\tilde{S}_{t+1}+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}.

(111)

Given this decomposition, we characterize the growth rate of
\lambda_{\min}(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top})
by first resorting to a more tractable lower bound. In particular, we define
M:=\left(I+\Xi\right)\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(I+\Xi\right)^{\top}
, where
\Xi:=V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Based on the definition of
M
, a series of inequalities analogous to (
91
) can be used to show that
\lambda_{\min}(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top})\geq\lambda_{\min}(M)
. Therefore, it suffices to provide a lower bound for
\lambda_{\min}(M)
. Similar to the proof of Proposition
4
, we first show that
\lambda_{\min}(M)\approx\lambda_{\min}(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top})
:

\displaystyle\left|\lambda_{\min}(M)-\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 3\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)\leq 192\sqrt{r}\eta\rho^{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right).

Combining the above inequality with the one-step dynamic of
\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)
from Proposition
2
completes the proof.
\hfill\square

E.3
Proof of Proposition
8

Similar to the minimum eigenvalue dynamics, the proof is identical to the proof of Proposition
5
. Hence, we only provide a sketch. Similar to (
94
), one can write

\displaystyle\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}=\Sigma-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}{-V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}-\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V-V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V}.

Lemma
29
combined with an argument similar to Appendix
D.3
leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V\right\rVert\leq 193\sqrt{r}\eta\rho^{t}\sigma_{1}\delta.

The above bound combined with the one-step dynamics of
\Sigma-\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}
in Proposition
3
completes the proof for the one-step dynamics of
\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}
. The dynamics of the cross term (
27
) and the upper bound on
\left\lVert S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert
(
28
) can be deduced in a similar fashion. The details are omitted for brevity.
\hfill\square

E.4
Proof of Proposition
9

The proof of Proposition
9
is identical to that of Proposition
6
, with a key difference that
\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq 16\sqrt{r}\eta\rho^{t}
. The details are omitted for brevity.
\hfill\square

E.5
Proof of Lemma
9

The proof is based on an inductive argument similar to the proof of Lemma
7
in Appendix
D.6
. Due to our special initialization, it is easy to verify that the statements of the lemma are satisfied for
t=0
. Now suppose that (
56
)-(
61
) are satisfied for
t
. Due to Proposition
9
, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+5\eta^{2}\rho^{2t}+49\sqrt{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}\delta\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert,

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left({5\eta^{2}\rho^{2t}+49\sqrt{r}\eta\rho^{t}\delta}\right)\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert,

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert\prod_{s=0}^{t}\exp\left({5\eta^{2}\rho^{2s}+49\sqrt{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{s}\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert\exp\left({\sum_{s=0}^{t}5\eta^{2}\rho^{2s}+49\sqrt{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{s}\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert\exp\left({\frac{5\eta^{2}}{1-\rho^{2}}+\frac{49\sqrt{r}\eta\delta}{1-\rho}}\right).

Due to
\rho=1-\Theta(\eta/(\kappa\log(1/\alpha)))
and
\eta\lesssim 1/(\kappa\log(1/\alpha))
, we have

\frac{5\eta^{2}}{1-\rho^{2}}\lesssim\eta\kappa\log(1/\alpha)\leq 1/2,\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{49\sqrt{r}\eta\delta}{1-\rho}\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta\log(1/\alpha).

Combining the above inequalities leads

\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq 2\left\lVert G_{0}\right\rVert\alpha^{-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta)}\leq 2\sqrt{2}\alpha^{1-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta)}\sqrt{\bar{\varphi}\delta},

where the last inequality is due to our special initialization technique and Lemma
6
. The remaining bounds in Lemma
9
can be established similar to Lemma
7
. The details are omitted for brevity.
\hfill\square

Appendix F
Omitted Proofs

F.1
Proof of Lemma
19

To prove this lemma, we use one-step discretization technique. First note that, for any
X\in\mathcal{S}_{k,\varepsilon}
, there exists a matrix in
X^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}_{k}
such that
\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq{\varepsilon}
. Suppose that
\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}
is a
\xi
-net of
\mathcal{S}_{k}
where
\xi\geq\varepsilon
. Based on the definition of
\xi
-net, there exists
X^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}
such that
\left\lVert X^{\prime}-X^{\prime\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\xi
. This implies that
\left\lVert X-X^{\prime\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert X^{\prime}-X^{\prime\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi
, and hence,
\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}
is a
2\xi
-net of
\mathcal{S}_{k,\varepsilon}
. Given this fact, one can write the following chain of inequalities for every
Y\in\mathbb{S}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{G}_{Y}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{X^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle-\varphi(X^{\prime})\left\langle\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}},Y\right\rangle\right]}_{{(A)}}

(112)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)-\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)\right)\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle\right]}_{(B)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\langle\varphi(X)\frac{X}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}}-\varphi(X^{\prime})\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}},Y\right\rangle}_{(C)}.

We control each term in the above inequality separately.

Bounding
{(A)}
.

To control
{(A)}
, note that
\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle-\varphi(X^{\prime})\left\langle\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}},Y\right\rangle
is
\mathcal{O}\left(1/m\right)
-sub-Gaussian and
{(A)}
is the supremum of the sub-Gaussian random variable over a the finite set
\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}
. Hence, the Maximum Inequality implies that

{(A)}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{dk}{m}\log\left(\frac{R}{\xi}\right)}.

(113)

Bounding
{(B)}
.

Invoking Hölder’s inequality, one can write

\displaystyle{(B)}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left|\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)-\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)\right|\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right].

Note that if
|\left\langle A_{i},X-X^{\prime}\right\rangle|\leq|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}-\lambda s_{i}\right\rangle|
, then
\mathrm{Sign}(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-\lambda s_{i})=\mathrm{Sign}(\left\langle A_{i},X\right\rangle-\lambda s_{i})
. Therefore, the above term can be further bounded by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left|\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)-\mathrm{Sign}\left(\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}\right)\right|\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

(114)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X-X^{\prime}\right\rangle|\geq|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X-X^{\prime}\right\rangle|\geq t\right)+\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X-X^{\prime}\right\rangle|\geq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]}_{(B_{1})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{X^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}}\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]}_{(B_{2})}.

For
(B_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle(B_{1})
