\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\frac{t}{2\xi}\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

(115)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\frac{t}{2\xi}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{j\neq i}|\left\langle A_{j},Y\right\rangle|\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\frac{t}{2\xi}\right)|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(e^{-C\frac{t^{2}}{\xi^{2}}}\sqrt{\log\left(m\right)}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\frac{t}{2\xi}\right)|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right],

where
C>0
is a universal constant and
{t}/{\xi}\geq\sqrt{d}
. Furthermore, applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq\frac{t}{2\xi}\right)|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{P}\left(2\xi\left\lVert A_{i}\right\rVert_{F}\geq t\right)}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle^{2}\right]}\lesssim e^{-C\frac{t^{2}}{\xi^{2}}}.

(116)

Hence, we conclude that
(B_{1})\lesssim e^{-C\frac{t^{2}}{\xi^{2}}}\sqrt{\log\left(m\right)}
.

Now we turn to bound
{(B_{2})}
. Note that
\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|
is
\mathcal{O}\left(\log(m)/m\right)
-sub-Gaussian, and
\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}
is a finite set. Hence, the Maximum Inequality yields

\displaystyle{(B_{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{X^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{k,\xi}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]+\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{dk\log(m)\log\left(R/\xi\right)}{m}}\right).

(117)

For the first part, one can write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right)\max_{1\leq i\leq m}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

(118)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{j\neq i}|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(|\left\langle A_{i},X^{\prime}\right\rangle-s_{i}|\leq t\right)|\left\langle A_{i},Y\right\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\log(m)}\frac{t}{\zeta}.

Therefore, we conclude that
(B)\lesssim\sqrt{\log(m)}\frac{t}{\zeta}+e^{-C\frac{t^{2}}{\xi^{2}}}\sqrt{\log\left(m\right)}+\sqrt{\frac{dk\log(m)\log\left(R/\xi\right)}{m}}
.
Finally, it remains to bound
(C)
in (
112
).

Bounding
(C)
.

We have

\displaystyle(C)

\displaystyle=\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\{\left(\varphi(X)-\varphi(X^{\prime})\right)\left\langle\frac{X}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}},Y\right\rangle+\varphi(X^{\prime})\left\langle\frac{X}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}}-\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}},Y\right\rangle\right\}

(119)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\{\varphi(X)-\varphi(X^{\prime})\right\}+\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\lVert\frac{X}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}}-\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert_{F}.

For the first part, we use Mean Value Theorem to write

|\varphi(X^{\prime})-\varphi(X)|\leq\left\lVert\nabla\varphi(Z)\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert X^{\prime}-X\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\left\lVert\nabla\varphi(Z)\right\rVert_{F}\xi,

(120)

where
Z=\lambda X+(1-\lambda)X^{\prime}
,
\lambda\in[0,1]
. Note that
\nabla\varphi(Z)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}p\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{s^{2}Z}{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{4}}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{2\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}}\right]
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}\geq\zeta}\left\lVert\nabla\varphi(Z)\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}\geq\zeta}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{s^{2}}{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{3}}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{2\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}}\right]\leq\frac{1}{\zeta}\sup_{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}\geq\zeta}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{s^{2}}{\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{2\left\lVert Z\right\rVert_{F}^{2}}}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{\zeta}.

(121)

For the second part, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\lVert\frac{X}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}}-\frac{X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\left\lVert X-X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 2\xi}\left\lVert\frac{X-X^{\prime}}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert_{F}+\left\lVert\frac{X\left(\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}-\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}\right)}{\left\lVert X\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert X^{\prime}\right\rVert_{F}}\right\rVert_{F}

(122)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\xi}{\zeta}.

Therefore, we conclude that
(C)\lesssim\frac{\xi}{\zeta}
.

Combining the derived upper bounds for
(A)
,
(B)
, and
(C)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{G}_{Y}]

\displaystyle\leq(A)+(B)+(C)

(123)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{dk}{m}\log\left(\frac{R}{\xi}\right)}+\sqrt{\log(m)}\frac{t}{\zeta}+e^{-C\frac{t^{2}}{\xi^{2}}}\sqrt{\log\left(m\right)}+\sqrt{\frac{dk\log(m)\log\left(R/\xi\right)}{m}}+\frac{\xi}{\zeta},

provided that
\xi\geq\varepsilon
and
t/\xi\geq\sqrt{d}
. Let
\xi\asymp\zeta\sqrt{{k}/{m}}
and
t\asymp\zeta\sqrt{{dk}\log\left(m\right)/{m}}
. Clearly, these choices of parameters satisfy
t/\xi\geq\sqrt{d}
. Moreover,
\xi\asymp\zeta\sqrt{{k}/{m}}
together with the assumption
\varepsilon\lesssim\zeta\sqrt{k/m}
implies that
\varepsilon\leq\xi
. Finally, plugging these values in (
123
) leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{G}_{Y}]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{dk}{m}\log^{2}(m)\log\left(\frac{R}{\zeta}\right)},

for every
Y\in\mathbb{S}
. This in turn implies

\displaystyle\sup_{Y\in\mathbb{S}_{k}}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{G}_{Y}]\leq\sup_{Y\in\mathbb{S}}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{G}_{Y}]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{dk}{m}\log^{2}(m)\log\left(\frac{R}{\zeta}\right)},

which completes the proof.
\hfill\square

F.2
Proof of Lemma
23

To prove
0.5\leq\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 2
, it suffices to show that
0.5\leq\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)\leq\left\lVert\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 2
. For brevity, we only show
0.5\leq\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)
, as the other part of the inequality can be proven in a similar fashion. One can write

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}

(124)

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right).

Note that the eigenvalues of
\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
are real and nonnegative, due to its similarity to
\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1/2}\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1/2}
. One the other hand, one can write

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=I+\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma+\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

We will show that every term in the above decomposition, except for the first term, is in the order of
\mathcal{O}(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1})
. First note that

\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma\right\rVert=\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\Sigma\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=\mathcal{O}(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}).

(125)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert 2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

(126)

\displaystyle=2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}S_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}).

It is easy to see that all the remaining terms are in the order of
\mathcal{O}(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1})
; we omit their proofs for brevity.
Combining the above bounds, we obtain

\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)=1-\mathcal{O}(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1})\geq 1/2,

(127)

where the last inequality is due to our choice of
\bar{\eta}_{t}
. This in turn implies that
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 2
. Similarly, we can show that
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 3
, by proving
\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})^{-1}\right)\geq 1/3
. This can be shown in an analogous fashion, after noting that

S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}=\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+\underbrace{V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}+\tilde{S}_{t+1}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{t}U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}V}_{\text{perturbation}}.

(128)

Similar to the proof of Proposition
5
, it can shown that the norm of the perturbation term is upper bounded by
4\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 1/6
, due to our choice of
\delta
and
\eta
, and our assumptions on
\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert
.

Finally, we prove the second statement by providing an upper bound for
\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
. Due to the first part of the lemma, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(\tilde{S}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t})S_{t}^{\top}

(129)

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t})S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}S^{\top}_{t}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)E^{\top}_{t}E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}.

Let us define
H_{t}=E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Based on this definition, one can verify that

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{t+1}\tilde{S}_{t+1}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=H_{t}+\bar{\eta}_{t}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}-\bar{\eta}_{t}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}H_{t}

(130)

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}H_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)E^{\top}_{t}H_{t}

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

Now, note that

\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top})\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert.

(131)

Similarly, we have
\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}H_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert
. Moreover, we have

\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}H_{t}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{2}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\rVert\lesssim(\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{1})^{2}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert.

(132)

In a similar fashion, it can be shown that
\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}\left(S_{t}^{\top}S_{t}+E_{t}^{\top}E_{t}\right)E^{\top}_{t}H_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert
and
\left\lVert\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}H_{t}S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{12}\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert
. Combining the derived bounds completes the proof.

\hfill\square

F.3
Proof of Lemma
24

To prove this lemma, we show that

\displaystyle S_{t+1}N_{t}=(I+\Xi)S_{t}N_{t}

(133)

for some matrix
\Xi
with
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert<1
. Before proceeding, we show that the above inequality is enough to prove the invertibility of
S_{t+1}N_{t}
. First note that
S_{t}N_{t}=M_{t}D_{t}
. Therefore, the matrix
M_{t}D_{t}
is invertible due to the assumption
S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\succ 0
. On the other hand,
\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert<1
implies that
I+\Xi
is invertible, thereby completing the proof.
To verify (
133
), it suffices to show that
\Xi=S_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}-I
has norm less than one. To this goal, we write

\displaystyle\Xi

\displaystyle=S_{t+1}N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}-I

\displaystyle=V^{\top}\left(U_{t}-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)U_{t}+R_{t}U_{t}\right)N_{t}\left(V^{\top}U_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}-\left(V^{\top}U_{t}N_{t}\right)\left(V^{\top}U_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=V^{\top}\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{t}\right)U_{t}N_{t}\left(V^{\top}U_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{=}}V^{\top}\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{t}\right)U_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=V^{\top}\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{t}\right)V+V^{\top}\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{t}\right)V_{\perp}H_{t},

where
H_{t}
is defined as
E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. Moreover, in (a), we used the following chain of equalities

N_{t}\left(V^{\top}U_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}=N_{t}\left(S_{t}N_{t}\right)^{-1}=N_{t}D_{t}^{-1}M_{t}^{\top}=N_{t}D_{t}M_{t}^{\top}M_{t}D_{t}^{-2}M_{t}^{\top}=S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

Therefore, we have

\left\lVert\Xi\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{t}\right\rVert\left(1+\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\right){\leq 2\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert+6\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\leq 1/2.

(134)

Here we used Lemma
26
,
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\lesssim\sigma_{1}
,
\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\leq 3\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}
,
\left\lVert H_{t}\right\rVert\leq 1/3
, and our assumption on
\eta
. This completes the proof.
\hfill\square

F.4
Proof of Lemma
27

First, note that

\displaystyle E_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top})-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{s}(S_{s}^{\top}S_{s}+E_{s}^{\top}E_{s})S_{s}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S^{\top}_{s}

(135)

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}(S_{s}^{\top}S_{s}+E_{s}^{\top}E_{s})S_{s}^{\top}\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}\left(S_{s}^{\top}S_{s}+E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}\right)E^{\top}_{s}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}

\displaystyle+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}S^{\top}_{s+1}+E_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V.

On the other hand, one can write

\displaystyle S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)+\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}

(136)

\displaystyle+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}S_{s+1}^{\top}+S_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V.

Pre-multiplying (
136
) with
H_{s}
leads to a relationship between
S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}
and
E_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}
:

\displaystyle E_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{T}=H_{s}S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}+T

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle H_{s+1}=H_{s}+T(S_{s+1}S_{s+1})^{-1},

where simple algebra reveals that

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=-H_{s}\left(\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)\right.

(137)

\displaystyle\left.+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-2\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}

\displaystyle+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}S^{\top}_{s+1}+E_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V

\displaystyle-H_{s}\left(V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}S_{s+1}^{\top}+S_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V\right).

For simplicity, we define
D=S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
in the sequel. Based on the the definition of
T
, one can write

\displaystyle H_{s}+T(S_{s+1}S_{s+1})^{-1}=H_{s}A_{s}+B_{s}+C_{s},

(138)

where the matrices
A_{s}
,
B_{s}
, and
C_{s}
are defined as

\displaystyle A_{s}=

\displaystyle I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma D-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)\Sigma\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)^{-1}D+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}D,

\displaystyle B_{s}=

\displaystyle\ -2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\Sigma\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)^{-1}D

\displaystyle-\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}D-\left(V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}S_{s+1}^{\top}+S_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V\right)\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle C_{s}=

\displaystyle-2\bar{\eta}_{t}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D

\displaystyle+\left(V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}S^{\top}_{s+1}+E_{s+1}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V+V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V\right)\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

To provide an upper bound on
\left\lVert A_{s}\right\rVert
, we define
P=\left(I+\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\Sigma\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)^{-1}-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)D
and
Q=I+\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\Sigma\left(S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\right)^{-1}-\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert A_{s}\right\rVert^{2}=\left\lVert I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma P\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\max}\left(\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}P\Sigma\right)^{\top}\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma P\right)\right)

(139)

\displaystyle\leq 1+\lambda_{\max}\left(-\bar{\eta}_{t}\Sigma P-\bar{\eta}_{t}P^{\top}\Sigma+\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}P^{\top}\Sigma^{2}P\right)

\displaystyle=1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Sigma P+P^{\top}\Sigma-\bar{\eta}_{t}P^{\top}\Sigma^{2}P\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1-\bar{\eta}_{t}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Sigma P+P^{\top}\Sigma-P^{\top}\Sigma P\right),

where we used the fact that
\eta\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{1}}
in the last inequality. Now it suffices to provide a lower bound for
\lambda_{\min}\left(\Sigma P+P^{\top}\Sigma-P^{\top}\Sigma P\right)
.
To this goal, we use the following intermediate lamma.

Lemma 30

(Theorem 4.1. in
Eisenstat and Ipsen 1998
)
.

Given a diagonal matrix
\Lambda\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
and its perturbed variant
\Lambda^{\prime}=\Lambda R
for some
R\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, we have

\min_{k}{|\lambda_{i}(\Lambda^{\prime})-\lambda_{k}(\Lambda)|}\leq|\lambda_{i}(\Lambda^{\prime})|\left\lVert I-R^{-1}\right\rVert.

(140)

for every
i=1,2,\cdots,d
.

To apply this lemma, we choose
\Lambda=\Sigma
and
R=P+\Sigma^{-1}P^{\top}\Sigma-\Sigma^{-1}P^{\top}\Sigma P
, which leads to the equality
\Sigma P+P^{\top}\Sigma-P^{\top}\Sigma P=\Sigma R
. Given this definition and Lemma
30
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{r}}{1+\left\lVert I-R^{-1}\right\rVert}\leq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma R).

(141)

Now, we provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert I-R^{-1}\right\rVert
. First note that
\left\lVert I-R^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert I-R\right\rVert\left\lVert R^{-1}\right\rVert
. On the other hand

\displaystyle\left\lVert I-R\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\Sigma^{-1}\left(P^{\top}-I\right)\Sigma\left(I-P\right)\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert I-P\right\rVert^{2}\leq\left(\left\lVert I-D\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(I-Q\right)D\right\rVert\right)^{2}.

Using a similar approach to the proof of Lemma
23
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert I-D\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 0.1.

On the other hand, one can write

\displaystyle\left\lVert(I-Q)D\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert I-Q\right\rVert\left\lVert D\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}3\left\lVert I-Q\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 0.1,

where, in (a), we used Lemma
23
. Therefore, we have
\left\lVert I-R\right\rVert\leq 0.04
. Next, we provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert R^{-1}\right\rVert
. Note that

\displaystyle R=P+\Sigma^{-1}P^{\top}\Sigma\left(I-P\right).

By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(R)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}(P)-\left\lVert\Sigma^{-1}P^{\top}\Sigma\left(I-P\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}(P)-\left\lVert P\right\rVert\left\lVert I-P\right\rVert

\displaystyle\geq 0.8-1.2\times 0.2=0.56.

Here we used the fact that
\left\lVert I-P\right\rVert\leq 0.2
. The above inequality implies that
\left\lVert R^{-1}\right\rVert=1/\sigma_{\min}(R)\leq 2
. Combining the above bounds, we have

\left\lVert I-R^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert I-R\right\rVert\left\lVert R^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 0.08.

This together with (
141
) implies that

\lambda_{\min}\left(\Sigma R\right)\geq 0.92\sigma_{r}.

Therefore, we have

\left\lVert A_{s}\right\rVert^{2}\leq 1-0.92\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}\quad\implies\quad\left\lVert A_{s}\right\rVert\leq 1-0.46\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}.

(142)

Next, we provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert B_{s}\right\rVert
. Simple algebra reveals that

\displaystyle\left\lVert 2\bar{\eta}_{t}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D\!+\!\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D\!+\!\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\Sigma S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}H_{s}D\!+\!\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\Sigma\left(\!S_{s}S_{s}^{\top}\!\right)^{-1}\!\!D\!+\!\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}E_{s}S_{s}^{\top}D\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert.

Next, we provide a bound for the remaining terms in
B_{s}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert R_{s}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{s}\right)^{-1}U_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert R_{s}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(I-\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}-X^{\star}\right)+R_{s}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert U_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert U_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert.

To proceed, we provide an upper bound for
\left\lVert U_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
.

\displaystyle\left\lVert U_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert VS^{\top}_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+V_{\perp}E_{s+1}S^{\top}_{s+1}\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 1+\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert.

Similarly, one can show that

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{\top}_{\perp}R_{s}U_{s}U_{s}^{\top}R_{s}^{\top}V\left(S_{s+1}S_{s+1}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}^{2}\delta^{2}\bar{\varphi}^{4}\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\left(1+\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert\right)\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}.

Combining the derived bounds leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert B_{s}\right\rVert\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}(1+\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert).

(143)

In a similar way, one can show that

\displaystyle\left\lVert C_{s}\right\rVert\lesssim\bar{\eta}_{t}\left\lVert E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert+\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}(1+\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert).

(144)

Substituting (
142
), (
143
), and (
144
) in (
138
) yields

\displaystyle(1-c_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F})\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert\leq\left(1-0.46\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}+c_{2}\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(\left\lVert S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)+c_{3}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt+c_{4}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert\leq\left(\frac{1-0.46\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r}+c_{2}\bar{\eta}_{t}\left(\left\lVert S_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert E_{s}E_{s}^{\top}\right\rVert\right)+c_{3}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}}{1-c_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}}\right)\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert+\frac{c_{4}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}}{1-c_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta\left\lVert\Delta_{s}\right\rVert_{F}}

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert H_{s+1}\right\rVert\leq(1-c_{5}\bar{\eta}_{t}\sigma_{r})\left\lVert H_{s}\right\rVert+c_{6}\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}\bar{\eta}_{t}\delta,

where the last inequality follows from the assumed upper bound on
\delta
, as well as (
43
)-(
45
). This completes the proof.
\hfill\square

F.5
Proof of Lemma
10

We first prove the upper bound. One can write

\displaystyle\log\left(\prod_{t=0}^{T}\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T}\log\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\leq\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\alpha\rho^{t}=\frac{\alpha}{1-\rho}.

Hence, we have
\prod_{t=0}^{T}\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\leq\exp\left(\frac{\alpha}{1-\rho}\right)
. For the lower bound, we have

\displaystyle\log\left(\prod_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T}\log\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\geq\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{\alpha\rho^{t}}{1+\alpha\rho^{t}}\geq\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{\alpha\rho^{t}}{1+\alpha}\geq\frac{\alpha}{1+\alpha}T\rho^{T}.

Hence, we have
\prod_{t=0}^{T}\left(1+\alpha\rho^{t}\right)\geq\exp\left(\frac{\alpha}{1+\alpha}T\rho^{T}\right)
.
\hfill\square

F.6
Proof of Lemma
11

We start with the proof of the first statement. We consider two cases:

-

Suppose that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\leq 0.01\sigma_{r}\rho^{t}
. Recall that
U_{t+1}=U_{t}-\frac{\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}U_{t}+R_{t}U_{t}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{t+1}=\left(U_{t}-\frac{\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\Delta_{t}U_{t}+R_{t}U_{t}\right)\left(U_{t}^{\top}-\frac{\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}U_{t}^{\top}\Delta_{t}^{\top}+U_{t}^{\top}R_{t}^{\top}\right)-X^{\star}.

The above equality leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Delta_{t+1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert+\eta\rho^{t}\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left(2+2\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert\right)

(145)

\displaystyle+2\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\left\lVert R_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+\eta^{2}\rho^{2t}\left\lVert U_{t}U_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert+4\sigma_{1}\eta\rho^{t}+4\sigma_{1}\eta\delta\rho^{t}\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert_{F}+2\sigma_{1}\eta^{2}\rho^{2t}

\displaystyle\leq 0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t}.

On the other hand, we know that
\gamma_{t+1}\leq 5\left\lVert\Delta_{t+1}\right\rVert
, which, together with the above inequality, implies that
\gamma_{t+1}\leq 0.1\sigma_{r}
.

-

Suppose that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\geq 0.01\sigma_{r}\rho^{t}
. Therefore, we have
\frac{\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{1}}
. On the other hand, since
\gamma_{t}\leq 0.1\sigma_{r}
, we have

\lambda_{\min}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)\geq 0.9\sigma_{r},\quad\left\lVert S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 1.1\sigma_{1},\quad\left\lVert E_{t}E_{t}^{\top}\right\rVert\leq 0.1\sigma_{r},\quad\left\lVert E_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\left(S_{t}S_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\leq 0.2.

This implies that the assumptions of Propositions
7
,
8
, and
9
are satisfied at iteration
t
, and we have

\displaystyle\gamma_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Sigma-S_{t+1}S_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+2\left\lVert S_{t+1}E_{t+1}^{\top}\right\rVert+\left\lVert F_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\left(1-\Omega(1)\frac{\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\gamma_{t}}\right)\gamma_{t}+\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{r}\sigma_{1}\eta_{0}\delta\rho^{t}+\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)\frac{\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{t}-\Omega\left(\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}\frac{\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}}{\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}\gamma_{t}-\Omega\left(\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}/\sqrt{d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{t}-\Omega\left(\sigma_{r}\eta_{0}\rho^{t}\right),

where
(a)
follows from the one-step dynamics of the signal, cross, and residual terms derived in Propositions
7
,
8
, and
9
. Moreover,
(b)
follows from
\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert\geq\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t}\right\rVert^{2}
. Therefore, we have
\gamma_{t+1}\leq\gamma_{t}\leq 0.1\sigma_{r}
.

To complete the proof of this lemma, it suffices to show that if
\left\lVert\Delta_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{d}\left\lVert G_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}
, then
\left\lVert\Delta_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{d}\alpha^{1-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta)}
. Note that based on our assumption and Phase 1 of the proof of Theorem
8
, the one-step dynamic of
G_{s}
holds for every
0\leq s\leq t+1
. Therefore, an analysis similar to Lemma (
9
) leads to
\left\lVert\Delta_{t+1}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{d}\alpha^{1-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{r}\kappa\delta)}
.
\hfill\square

F.7
Proof of Lemma
12

Since
\left\lVert\Delta_{t_{0}+T_{3}-1}\right\rVert\leq 0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}-1}
, an argument similar to the proof of Lemma
11
can be invoked to show that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t_{0}+T_{3}}\right\rVert\leq 0.03\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}}
and
\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}}\leq 0.15\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}}
. Let
\Delta t
be the first time that
\left\lVert\Delta_{t_{0}+T_{3}+\Delta t}\right\rVert\leq 0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}+\Delta t}
. Note that since
\left\lVert\Delta_{t_{0}+T_{3}}\right\rVert>0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}}
, we have
\Delta t\geq 1
. This implies that, for every
0\leq s\leq\Delta t-1
, we have
\left\lVert\Delta_{t_{0}+T_{3}+s}\right\rVert>0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}+s}
. Therefore, (
-
‣
F.6
) implies that
\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}+s+1}\leq\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}+s}-\Omega(\sigma_{r}\eta\rho^{t_{0}+s})
. This in turn leads to

\displaystyle\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}+\Delta t}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}}-\Omega\left(\sum_{s=0}^{\Delta t-1}\sigma_{r}\eta\rho^{t_{0}+s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 0.15\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}}-\Omega\left(\sum_{s=0}^{\Delta t-1}\sigma_{r}\eta\rho^{t_{0}+s}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}}\left(0.15-\Omega\left(\sum_{s=0}^{\Delta t-1}\eta\rho^{s}\right)\right).

Let us assume that
\Delta t\lesssim(\kappa/\eta)\log(1/\alpha)
. Under this assumption, we have
\rho^{s}=\Omega(1)
for every
s\leq\Delta t
. This implies that
\Omega\left(\sum_{s=0}^{\Delta t-1}\eta\rho^{s}\right)=\Omega(\eta\Delta t)
. Therefore, upon choosing
\Delta t=\Omega(1/\eta)
, we have
\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}+\Delta t}\leq 0.15-\Omega\left(\sum_{s=0}^{\Delta t-1}\eta\rho^{s}\right)\leq 0
. This implies that, there must exist
\tilde{t}\leq\Delta t
such that
\gamma_{t_{0}+T_{3}+\tilde{t}}\leq 0.02\sigma_{r}\rho^{t_{0}+\tilde{t}}
. This completes the proof.
\hfill\square
